# Agents · P · part 10

950 entries, pr-file-analyst to pre-dev-validator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pr-file-analyst**: Analyzes a single file of a GitHub pull request for review. Given the file's diff, content, and graph neighbors, returns a structured JSON enrichment: a plain-language summary, the "why" of each connection, and review insights (risks, suspected bugs, tests to check, impact). Dispatched in parallel (one per file, or per small batch) by the /pr-map skill. ([ValentinDutra/pr-map](https://github.com/ValentinDutra/pr-map/blob/HEAD/pr-map-plugin/agents/pr-file-analyst.md) · ★9)
- **pr-finalizer**: 実装タスクと skeptical-reviewer が完了した直後に起動する。現在のブランチの PR を対象に、①CI の成功確認（失敗なら原因を報告）、②PR 説明文の内容確認と日英併記への更新、の 2 つを行う。コードは変更しない。 ([ocknamo/kanabun](https://github.com/ocknamo/kanabun/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-finalizer.md))
- **pr-finalizer**: Posts the run's assembled review detail to a finished PR as chained comments, then writes the run's final section into the PR body — what the review rounds found, what changed in response, and the scope notes the run settled — replacing the section a previous run left, so re-running it stacks nothing. Given a PR number, a branch name, and those scope notes, reads the rounds out of the PR's XDG state directory and the commits off the branch, posts the detail, and amends the body once per run. The only agent that edits a PR body. Spawned by /sdlc:orchestrate after the review loop ends and befor… ([TheVoskamps/claude-plugins-marketplace](https://github.com/TheVoskamps/claude-plugins-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/pr-finalizer.md))
- **pr-finding-verifier**: Takes a single adversarial review finding and tries to prove it wrong against the code at head. The counterweight to pr-adversary — sympathetic to the change, sceptical of the finding. Read-only. ([DeepBlueCLtd/drogna](https://github.com/DeepBlueCLtd/drogna/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-finding-verifier.md))
- **pr-finish**: Prepare a narrow myK9 change for review by checking verification, tracking docs, branch hygiene, and unresolved launch-readiness risk. ([rbeezley/myk9-platform](https://github.com/rbeezley/myk9-platform/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/pr-finish.md) · ★1)
- **pr-fix-agent**: Fix STP/STD documents based on PR review comments from humans or bots. Classifies comments as auto-fixable or needs-human, applies fixes using QualityFlow skills, validates results, and pushes updated commits. ([redhat-community-ai-tools/qualityflow](https://github.com/redhat-community-ai-tools/qualityflow/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-fix-agent.md) · ★1)
- **pr-fix-executor**: 代码修复执行者，根据审核意见修复代码并提交。 适用于：用户选择自动修复或 team-lead 判定需要修复的场景。 **触发条件（auto-fix 可行的判断标准）**： - 所有阻塞项均为 MEDIUM 以下，或 HIGH 且修改面 &lt; 3 文件 - 不涉及架构重设计（handler 分层、配置结构重组等 CRITICAL 级别问题） - PR 分支有本地 worktree 或可以安全 checkout **不适用场景（应 REJECT 而非 auto-fix）**： - CRITICAL 阻塞涉及架构/分层违规（需作者重新设计） - 阻塞项 ≥ 5 个或修改面 &gt; 5 文件 - Scope 不诚实（未交付的目标需作者重新明确） 注意：此 agent 是项目特定的执行角色， 具有代码编辑和 git 操作能力。 ([jacobcy/vibe-coding-control-center](https://github.com/jacobcy/vibe-coding-control-center/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-fix-executor.md) · ★6)
- **pr-fixer**: Fix PR review feedback. Reads review comments, applies code fixes, commits and pushes. Use when PRs have review issues to resolve. ([ryaker/SparrowDB](https://github.com/ryaker/SparrowDB/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-fixer.md) · ★78)
- **pr-fixer**: Use to resolve findings on an existing pull request - review comments, CI failures, and self-review findings - fixing code, updating tests, re-verifying gates, and pushing. The counterpart to pr-reviewer that closes the loop. ([theam/claude-dev-kit](https://github.com/theam/claude-dev-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-fixer.md) · ★14)
- **pr-fixer**: Do not invoke directly or proactively — internal worker dispatched only by the coding-toolbox fresh-pr skill. Verifies CI-failure and CodeRabbit findings against the actual code, applies the justified fixes and commits them following repo conventions, and always annotates skipped findings in code to prevent re-flagging. ([kwitsch/claude-plugins](https://github.com/kwitsch/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/coding-toolbox/agents/pr-fixer.md) · ★1)
- **pr-fixer**: Subagent for applying PR fixes when there are many issues to address ([deployhq/claude-fix-pr](https://github.com/deployhq/claude-fix-pr/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-fixer.md))
- **pr-fixer**: PR Fixer — reads a PR's review comments (panel-posted or human), applies fixes scoped strictly to them, runs the submission gate, commits and pushes to the PR branch, and watches CI — iterating in a bounded loop until the PR is green, or stopping and reporting if it can't get there. The counterpart to the PR-review panel; the agent that "brings it to green." Invoke via /pr-fix &lt;PR#&gt; (see .pr-review/AGENT.md and .pr-review/README.md). ([jasgr-software/tax-portal](https://github.com/jasgr-software/tax-portal/blob/HEAD/.pr-review/agents/fixer.md))

([rubinder/wiz_scheduler](https://github.com/rubinder/wiz_scheduler/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-fixer.md))
- **pr-fixer**: &gt; Fixes issues identified by CodeRabbit, Reviewer, or Tester ([usorama/engg-support-system](https://github.com/usorama/engg-support-system/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-fixer.md))

- **pr-gate-runner**: Runs a repo's gate tasks on main and on a PR's head commit in a scratchpad clone, then reports baseline-diff classification with one execution evidence record per gate. Use when a pr-review reviewer needs gate evidence. Never judges the diff, never edits, never merges. ([vinnie357/claude-skills](https://github.com/vinnie357/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/tools/github/skills/pr-review/agents/pr-gate-runner.md) · ★25)
- **pr-generator**: 機能実装またはバグ修正完了後に包括的な Pull Request ドキュメントを生成する汎用エージェント。5フェーズ処理(Git収集→解釈→生成→レビュー→品質ゲート)とCodex MCPによるsynthesisでPR品質を保証。レビュー→再生成のフィードバックループ(最大2回)で出力を改善する。以下のシナリオで使用:\n\n- ユーザーが機能実装を完了し「機能を完成させました。PRを作成できますか?」のような発言をした場合\n- ユーザーが「PR の説明を生成して」「プルリクエストを作成して」のような明示的な要求をした場合\n- ユーザーが大幅な変更を行い「PRに何を書けばいい?」と質問した場合\n- 一連のコミット完了後、ユーザーが変更内容のドキュメント化支援を求めた場合\n\n使用例:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: ユーザーが新機能の実装を完了し、PRを作成したい\nuser: "新しい認証システムの実装が完了しました。プルリクエストを作成してもらえますか?"\nassistant: "5フェーズ処理でPRドラフトを生成します: Git収集→解釈→生成→レビュー→品質ゲート"\n&lt;commentary&gt;\nユーザーが作業完了後にPR作成を要求しているので、pr-generatorエージェントを起動してコミットを分析しPR説明を生成します。\n&lt;/commen… ([aglabo/idd-framework](https://github.com/aglabo/idd-framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/idd-framework/agents/pr-generator.md))
- **pr-generator**: Use this agent when you need to generate a pull request description from git diffs. The agent: - Checks if branch is in sync with main and rebases if needed - Intelligently selects the right diff based on context (last commit only vs entire branch) - Analyzes code changes and creates properly formatted PR descriptions - Detects feature flags, test coverage, breaking changes, documentation, and environment variables Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User just committed staged changes and wants PR based only on that commit. user: "commit staged changes then create PR based on it" &lt;commentary&gt; The us… ([thomfilg/ai-plugin-work](https://github.com/thomfilg/ai-plugin-work/blob/HEAD/plugins/work/agents/pr-generator.md))
- **pr-ghostwriter**: Kod değişikliklerinden PR açıklaması, commit mesajı ve changelog üretir. Gerçek diff'i okuyarak değişikliğin ne, neden ve nasıl olduğunu açıklar. Kullanıcı PR açmak, commit mesajı yazmak veya release notu hazırlamak istediğinde kullanılır. Jenerik açıklama üretmez — her zaman gerçek değişikliğe özgü yazar. ([komunite/kalfa](https://github.com/komunite/kalfa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-ghostwriter.md) · ★245)

- **pr-guard**: Use this agent for comprehensive pre-merge PR validation. - **pr-guardian**: Background guardian for ONE pushed branch. Dispatched by the pr-guardian plugin's PostToolUse hook after a push, or by `/pr-guardian watch`. Keeps the pull request mergeable end to end — rebases on the default branch and resolves conflicts, fixes what CI and the reviewers report, commits and pushes per the repo's configured policy, resyncs the PR body when asked to, and reports GREEN / MERGED / BLOCKED / UNREVIEWED with proof. Not for opening a PR on someone's behalf as a favour, not for reviewing someone else's PR. ([cdfarkas/cdfarkas-skills](https://github.com/cdfarkas/cdfarkas-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/pr-guardian/agents/pr-guardian.md))
- **pr-health-checker**: Use this agent when checking PR status for CI failures, merge conflicts, stale reviews, rebase status, or other health issues. - **pr-integrator**: Integrates open PRs into develop following best practices. For each PR (from a @pr-triager report or a single PR number) it updates the branch from origin/develop, verifies CI is green, and squash-merges behind confirmation gates, deleting the branch after. - **pr-invariant**: Lists the system's invariants and follows whether the change still holds them. ([lexijamesesq/publish-skills](https://github.com/lexijamesesq/publish-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: The merge gate. Review-only: it never edits code — it posts a `gh pr review` with actionable findings and a clear ship/no-ship decision. Use before merging ANY prepared PR. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-security.md))
- **pr-reviewer-sp**: PR-Reviewer SP: adversarial merge gate on the uncommitted git diff (staged included) — reject if not passing; traces real call chains to verify behavior, lands -pr-review.md via native edit/write and reports to the dispatcher. ([tanmoumou252/skill_toolkit](https://github.com/tanmoumou252/skill_toolkit/blob/HEAD/kilocode/agents/pr-reviewer-sp.md))
- **pr-reviewer-sp**: PR-Reviewer SP: adversarial merge gate on the uncommitted git diff (staged included) — reject if not passing; traces real call chains to verify behavior; reports land as -pr-review.md via controlled write_scoped_file/edit_scoped_file. - **pr-reviewer-summary**: Kata PR sub-reviewer. Invoked first by the /relay-pr-review skill so the consolidator can pin the summary at the top. - **pr-reviewer-tests**: Relay PR sub-reviewer (v2 inline pattern). Invoked by /relay-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api with marker &lt;!-- relay-review:tests --&gt;.
